FLIGHTMANUAL
1 APPLICABILITY
ALLLIMITATIONSSPECIFIEDINTHISSECTIONAREMANDATORY.
Theselimitationsareapplicableto thestandardaircraft
version.
Anyadditionallimitationsresultingfromtheinstallationof
optionalequipmentarespecifiedintheSUPPLEMENTSSection.
2 APPROVEDFLIGHTCONDITIONS
ThehelicopterisapprovedfordaytimeandnighttimeVFRandIFR
operation.
3 TYPESOF OPERATIONS
Whenequippedwiththeappropriateequipment,thehelicoptercanperform,
amongtheothers,thefollowingmissions
- Passengertransport.
- Loadcarrying(internalandexternal).
- Searchandrescue.
- Casualtyevacuation.
- Ferryflight.
- Allmissionsrequestingpodor sidemountedarmement. R
4 RESTRICTIONS
Thefollowingareprohibited:
·Aerobaticflying;
· Extendedrearwardflightliabletoallowexhaustgasesto
enterthecabin;
· Intentionalrunninglandingson softground;
·Deliberatepenetrationintocloudswithmassivevertical
development;
· Intentionalfullautorotationlandings;
Downwindflightwiththebleedvalveoffsetcontrolselectedoff
·UseofOEIenginepowerratingsexceptintheeventof an
actualsingleenginefailureandformaintenancepurposes;
Fueltransferbetweentankgroupsduringtakeoffor landing
· Flightin icingconditions.
NOTE Localicingphenomenamayoccurinhumidconditionswith
an indicatedoutsideairtemperatureof+3°C.

limitedto45°.

16 ENGINEOPERATINGlIMITATIONS
16.1 EngineStartingandInflightRelighting
Enginestartingandrelightingisauthorizedthroughoutthe
aircraftflightenvelope,exceptatOATsbelow-30°Cwherestarting
andrelightingarerestrictedto a maximumpressurealtitudeof
13130ft (4000m).
16.2 P2AirBleed
FOllOWINGAUTOMATICSHUTOFFOF THEP2AIRBLEEDSYSTEMAFTER
A SINGLE-ENGINEFAILURE,THEHEATINGANDDEMISTINGSYSTEMS
MAYCONTINUETO BE USEDPROVIDEDAIRCRAFTPERFORMANCE
REQUIREMENTSAREMETANDCOMPLIANCEWITHOEIlIMITATIONSIS
ENSURED.
16.3 EnginePowerApplicationatTemperaturesbelowO°C
- Afterstartingtheengines,maintainflightidleforthe
timesindicatedinthefollowingtable.
- Graduallyincreasethepowerwhilemonitoringtheoil
pressure.
- DO NOTAPPLYPOWERIFTHEENGINEallPRESSUREEXCEEDS87 PSI(6BAR)

below

16.4 Availabilityof DEIratings
Thefollowingchecks,describedinSection4 NormalProceduresmust
beperformedto ensureavailabilityofDEIHI andDEILOrating: R
- 4.2 § 10.2 Engineconditiondailychecksatisfactory.
- 4.1 § 4 CheckthatbothenginesGOVandPWRlightsare
extinguishedafterthecomputersself-test.
- 4.1 § 5.2 Checkarmingof DEIHI ratingsthenselectionof DEILO R
ratingsandcheckthattheyarearmedduring1stengine R
startup.
- 4.1 § 6 .Checkthattheatmosphericpressuredisplayedon thePFO
isconsistentwiththepressuregivenbythecontrol
towerwhensettingthealtimeters.
.Testboosterpumps.
.Checkthatbothboosterpumpsofeachenginearesetto
ON afterthetest.
- 4.1 § 8.1 CheckthattheGOVlightsareextinguishedduring
pre-flightchecks.
- 4.1 § 8.2 CheckenginealignmentandensurethatOIFFNG light
doesnotilluminateduringtransitiontohoverflight.
17 FLIGHTPARAMETERLIMITATIONS
17.1 FirstLimitIndicator(FLI)
Thefirstlimitindicatordisplaystheextentof thecurrent
limitingparameter(torque,Ng,t4, (exceptforDEIHIandDEILO R
rating),ENG1 or ENG2)on a scalegraduatedfromO to 10representing
thecollectivepitchlevertravelrange.

99-04 Page10

PDF PAGE 51
   
 
FLIGHT MANUAL 
  
   
17.2  MGB Torque   
   
      NOTE  : 100% torque corresponds to an MGB input of 2410 kW on  
             the 8000-rpm shaft at a rotor speed of 265 rpm (100% NR).  
   
      Twin-Engine Operations   
   
   
      - IAS > Vy  
        . Maximum continuous torque (unlimited duration)...........  77 %      R  
      - IAS < Vy  
        . Maximum continuous torque (unlimited duration)...........  77 %      R  
        . Maximum 5-min torque .................................... 100 %      R  
        . Maximum transient (20-s) twin-engine torque ............. 110 %      R  
   
      Single-Engine Operations   
   
      - Maximum continuous OEI torque (unlimited duration) .......  68.4 %  
      - Maximum OEI LO torque ....................................  70.6 %  
      - Maximum OEI HI torque ....................................  74.8 %  
   
17.3  Gas Generator Speed  (Ng)  
   
      Ng limitations vary according to the pressure altitude and outside air  
   
      temperature. The Ng limits in the following tables are specified as  
      percentage deviations from the takeoff rating (0).  
                                                                             
  |                                                                        |  
  |                       TWIN ENGINE OPERATIONS                           |  
  |                                                                        |  
  |                                                                        |  
  |   RATING                       TIME LIMIT           ∆ Ng LIMIT         |  
  |                                                                        |  
  |   Transient                    20 s                   + 1.3 %          |  
  |                                                                        |  
  |   Takeoff                      5 min                    0              |  
  |                                                                        |  
  |   Maximum continuous           unlimited              - 2.7 %          |  
  |                                                                        |  
  |                                                                        |  
  |                       SINGLE ENGINE OPERATIONS                         |  
  |                                                                        |  
  |                                                                        |  
  |   RATING                       TIME LIMIT           ∆ Ng LIMIT         |  
  |                                                                        |  
  |                                                                        |  
  |   OEI HI                       30 s                   + 4.5 %          |  
  |                                                                        |  
  |   OEI LO                       2 min                  + 2 %            |  
  |                                                                        |  
  |   Maximum continuous OEI       unlimited              + 1 %            |  
  |                                                                        |  
   
      In case of P2 air bleed for cabin or cockpit heating, the Both Engines  
      Operating and Maximum Continuous OEI limitations are to be reduced  
      by 1 %.

17.4  T4 Temperature   
   
      -  Engine starting :  
   
         . Maximum continuous t4 temperature ........................ 750°C  
         . Maximum transient (5-s) t4 temperature ................... 800°C  
         . Absolute maximum (2-s) t4 temperature .................... 810°C  
   
      - Both engines operating :  
   
         . Maximum t4 temperature at takeoff rating ................. 825°C  
         . Maximum t4 temperature at maximum continuous rating ...... 770°C  
   
      - One engine inoperative :  
   
         . Maximum t4 temperature at OEI LO rating .................. 870°C    R  
         . Maximum t4 temperature at maximum continuous OEI rating .. 840°C    R  
   
17.5  Main Rotor Speed  (NR)  
   
      Power-On Flight  
   
         . Nominal rotor speed ........................... 265 rpm (100 %)  
         . Maximum continuous rotor speed ................ 275 rpm (104 %)  
         . Minimum stabilized rotor speed ................ 245 rpm (92.5 %)  
         . Minimum transient rotor speed in flight ....... 220 rpm (83 %)  
   
      Power-Off Flight  
   
         . Maximum continuous rotor speed ................ 290 rpm (109.5 %)  
         . Maximum transient rotor speed (20 s max.) ..... 310 rpm (117 %)  
         . Minimum rotor speed at airspeeds below 100 kt . 220 rpm (83 %)  
         . Minimum rotor speed at airspeeds above 100 kt . 245 rpm (92.5 %)  
   
         NOTE  : Aural warning thresholds :  
   
                Overspeed ................................ 290 rpm (109.5 %)  
                Underspeed ............................... 249 rpm (94 %)  
   
17.6  Free Turbine Speed  (Nf)  
   
      The free turbine speed (Nf) is displayed on the triple RPM               R  
      indicator in equivalent rotor speed (NR), where NR 265 rpm (100%)        R  
      correspond to Nf 23 000 rpm.                                             R  
   
      Compliance with the NR limitations ensures that Nf limitations are  
      not exceeded.  
   
      NOTE : Engine overspeed shutdown ................ 318-326 rpm (120-123 %)

20 MISCELLANEOUSLIMITATIONS
20.1 ComponentswithServiceLifeLimits
Componentswithservicelifelimitsarelistedtogetherwiththe
specifiedlimitinSection5 of theMasterServicingRecommendations
(PRE)Revision3 andsubsequentRevisionsandmustbe replacedin
compliancewiththeserecommendations.
CAUTION:IFOATONTHEGROUNDOR INFLIGHTISBELOWMINUS30°C,TOTAL
TIMEOF FLIGHTSHALLBE RECORDEDSEPARATELYFORDETERMINING
TBOANDSERVICELIFELIMIT(REFERTOMASTERSERVICING
RECOMMENDATIONS- PRE).
20.2 Dailychecks
Thedailychecksbeforethefirstflightof thedayandturnaround
checksshouldbe carriedouteitherbythepilotincompliancewith
Section8 of theFlightManual,or by themechanicincompliancewith
theMasterServicingRecommendations(PRE).
20.3 Positionof PFDs
ThePFD(s)mustbeselectedon theouterscreensexceptinthereconfi-
gurationcasesdescribedinSection3.9.
20.4 Useof theCyclicChannelFlightDirector(F/DCYC)
Theuseof F/DCYCisprohibitedinsingle-axisconfiguration.

1 GENERAL
1.1 Wording
Thissectioncoversbasicemergenciesthatareindicatedbyaircraft
systemsor causethesymptomsdescribed.Forothertypesof failure
situations,thecrewmusttakeoperationalconditionsintoconsideration.
Thefollowingexpressionsareusedinthissectionaccordingtothedegree
of urgency:
- LAND(DITCH)IMMEDIATELY.
LANDAS SOONAS POSSIBLE
landingispossible.
Landatthenearestsitewherea safe
LIMITDURATIONOF FLIGHT:Extendedflightisnotrecommended the
landingsiteanddurationof flightareleftto thepilotis
discretion,takingtheoperationalenvironmentintoaccount.
1.2 WarningLightsRepetition
Theilluminationofa redwarninglightontheCautionAdvisoryPanel
causestheredALARMlightto illuminateon theinstrumentpanel.
- Theilluminationofanyellowlighton theCautionAdvisoryPanel
causestheyellowWARNlightto illuminateon theinstrumentpanel.
NOTE Theindicatingsystemmustbe resetafteranyfailureshownbythe
WARNorALARMlights.
1.3 IllustrationAccuracy
- Theillustrationsassociatedto somefailureconfigurationsare
meantto helpthecrewidentifythemainsymptomsofthefailure
underconsideration(theconsequencesofthosefailuresbefore
andaftercrewactionarenotrepresented); theseillustrations
mayalsorepresentthecontrolsappropriateto thoseaction.
- Theillustrationsshallnotbe consideredas a descriptionof the
helicopterinthatsomedetailscanbe different.
- Theindicatorsareonlyillustratedto showlimitations,
theunitsdo notnecessarilycorrespondto eachaircraftversion.
- Inparticular,theminorlayout evolutionswhichdo notimpairthe
interpretationofthefailuresymptomsor theunderstandingof a
systemlsoperationarenotsystematicallyshownintheillustrations.

5.2 OverWaterwithoutEmergencyFloatationSystem
Theprocedureisbasicallythesameason land,butwiththelanding
gearretracted.Otherdifferencesarenotedbelow:
- Opentheslidingdoorsduringtheapproachphase,
- Reduceairspeedasmuchaspossiblebeforetouchdown,
- Jettisontheemergencyexitsontouchdown,
- Maintaintheaircrafthorizontalusingthecyclicstick,
- Applytherotorbrakewithoutdecreasingthecollectivepitch,
- Do notunfastensafetybeltsuntiltheaircraftisimmersed. R
- Do notinflatelifejacketsuntilclearof theaircraft.
NOTE IncaseofemergencylandingonwaterwithoutemergencyfloatationR
system,thehelicopterturnsoverto a rotordownwardposition. R

allowingthetailskidto striketheground.

6.4 EngineFailureinHoverOGE
a) ImmediateLanding:
- ControlNR.
- Simultaneouslyincreaseairspeedto at least30 kt (ormoreif
aircraftheightpermits)by adoptinga nose-downattitudeof up
to 15°maximum.
- As aircraftapproachestheground,leveloffandreduceforward
airspeed.
- As aircraftbeginsto sink,controlattitudeandcushiontouchdown.
b)ContinuedFlight:
- Continuedflightmaybe possible.
6.5 EngineFailureinStabilizedFlight(Climb,LevelFlightorDescent)
a) ImmediateLanding:
- ControlNR.
- Adopta flightconfigurationcompatiblewithavailableenginepower.
- DisabletheDEIHI rating. R
- Followtherecommendedapproachpath.
- Prepareforsingle-enginelanding.
· Landinggear Down
· Nosewheel Locked
· Parkingbrake Released
· Failedengineboosterpumps Off
· Failedenginefuelflowcontrollever Shutdownposition
· EnabletheDEIHI ratingbeforelanding. R
b)ContinuedFlight:
- Continuedflightmaybe possible.
- Attemptto relight

FLIGHTMANUAL
6.6 EngineFailureduringApproachon RecommendedLandingPath
a) ImmediateLanding:
- ControlNR.
- Simultaneouslycontrolairspeed:below50 ft,maintainairspeedat
approximately30 ktuntilaircraftapproachestheground.
- Reduceforwardairspeedasaircraftapproachestheground.
-Asaircraftbeginsto sink,controlattitudeandcushiontouchdown.
NOTE: Forgrossweightuptomaximumtwinenginehoverperformance R
---- IGE10ft,maintainairspeedabove40 kt insteadof30 kt R
b)ContinuedFlight:
Continuedflightmaybepossibleby interruptingthelandingapproach,
providedtheaircraftheightabovethegroundisat least50 ftat
thetimeof theenginefailure,anddependingon theOEIperformance
on theinterruptedapproachpath.
- ControlNR.
- Simultaneouslygainclimbingspeed.
7 INTENTIONALSINGLE-ENGINESHUTDOWNAFTERMALFUNCTION
- DependingonDEIperformance,considerlimitingthedurationof the
flightor landingas soonas possible.
- ReducethecollectivepitchinaccordancewithDEIlimitations
(Reminder:minimumpowerairspeedisVy).
- Switchofftheheatinganddemistingsystems.
Movethefuelflowcontrolleverforthefailedenginetothefastidle
gate.
- Checkthatthefirstlimiton theremainingengineisnotexceeded,
whilemaintainingNR at250rpm(94%)or higher(adjustcollective
pitchifnecessary).
- Switchofftheboosterpumps.
- Movethefuelflowcontrollevertotheshutdownposition.
- DisabletheDEIHI rating(enablebeforelanding).
NOTE: Theheatinganddemistingsystemsareusableafterintentional
---- single-engineshutdownwithinrespectthelimitations.
Transferringfuelfromthefailedengine
- Failedenginefuelshutoffvalve Closed
- Failedengineboosterpumps On
- Transferpump On

The RCh supersedes the RCg.
Paragraph 7.1 is modified and completed as follows :                           R
Untimely illumination of an overspeed warning light                        R
     - Symptoms  :
       . Intermittent or continuous illumination of an | OVSP | warning light,
         without flame-out.
     - Actions  :
       Refer to SINGLE-ENGINE FAILURE (SECTION 3.1 PARAGRAPH 6)
       If the flight is to be continued
       - Move the fuel flow control lever for the affected engine to the
         fast idle gate.
       - Apply the INTENTIONAL SINGLE-ENGINE SHUTDOWN AFTER MALFUNCTION
         procedure for the affected engine
Paragraph 8 is modified as follows :
8 IN-FLIGHT ENGINE RELIGHTING
   The CAUTION is modified as follows :
CAUTION  : IN CASE OF INTENTIONAL SINGLE-ENGINE SHUTDOWN FURTHER TO THE
             UNTIMELY ILLUMINATION OF AN OVERSPEED WARNING LIGHT, DO NOT
             RELIGHT THE ENGINE.
    |                                                                    |
    | CAUTION  : THIS PAGE MUST NOT BE REMOVED FROM THE MANUAL UNTIL      |
    |           EMBODIMENT OF MODIFICATION 07.26071 (TU 205).            |

7.1  Failure of the free-turbine governing-system sensors of an engine           
  
     - Symptoms :                                                               
                               |         |      
       . Illumination of the   | DIFF NG |  warning light.                     
                               |         | 
       . Ng stabilized at idle.                                                 
       . Zero torque for the affected engine.              |       |              
       . In some cases, NR drop with illumination of the   | PWR 1 |  or       
         |       |                                         |       | 
         | PWR 2 |  warning light and activation of the MIN NR aural warning.  
         |       | 
                               |        |      
       . Illumination of the   |  GOV   |   warning light.                     R 
                               |        | 
     - Actions :                                                                 
       Refer to SINGLE-ENGINE FAILURE (SECTION 3.1 PARAGRAPH 6)                 
       For the affected engine, apply the procedure INTENTIONAL SINGLE-ENGINE    
       SHUTDOWN AFTER MALFUNCTION.                                               
  
7.2  Untimely illumination of an overspeed warning light                         
   
     - Symptoms :                                                                
       . Intermittent or continuous illumination of an | OVSP | warning light,   
         without flame-out or without automatic reduction of Ng at idle.         
   
     - Procedure :                                                                
       . As safety regarding with the double NF measure failure is ensured by    
         MOD 07 26071 (TU 205), the fuel governing can be left under normal      
         operation until the end of the flight.                                   
   
     - Action : CONTINUE FLIGHT                                                  
   
8  INFLIGHT ENGINE RELIGHTING 
  
   CAUTION : IN CASE OF INTENTIONAL SINGLE-ENGINE SHUT-DOWN FURTHER TO THE       
             FAILURE OF THE FREE-TURBINE GOVERNING-SYSTEM SENSORS, DO NOT        
             RELIGHT THE ENGINE.                                                 
  
   - Reduce airspeed to approximately Vy. 
   - If restarting can be attempted immediately : 
     . As soon as Ng reaches 10 %, start the engine using the normal 
       procedure. 
   - If restarting cannot be attempted immediately : 
     . It is necessary to wait for the residual t4 temperature to drop 
       down to 150°C or less, then : 
       . Booster pumps............................. ON 
       . Fuel flow control lever................... Check in OFF gate 
       . Starting pushbutton....................... . Press and hold for 10 to 
                                                      15 s then, without  
                                                      releasing the pushbutton, 
                                                      bring the lever into the 
                                                      starting gate. 
                                                    . Release the pushbutton. 
       . Check that normal starting sequence proceeds correctly. 
  
   NOTE : - If the engine MPAI bullet is in closed position 
            (anti-ice position) : 
            . leave the bullet in closed position, 
            . attempt relight in accordance with normal procedure.

     MGB Bay                                                                  
                                                                              
       - Symptoms  :

                               and LIMIT DURATION OF FLIGHT.

9.4  Fire in Engine Bay in Flight  * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * *  
     *                                                                       * 
     * - Symptoms :                                                          * 
     *                    |       |     |       |                            * 
     *   . Illumination : |FIRE 1 | or  |FIRE 2 | + AURAL WARNING            * 
     *                    |       |     |       |                            * 
     *                                                                       * 
     *       ILLUMINATION OF WARNING LIGHT ON FUEL SHUTOFF CONTROL           * 
     *                                                                       * 
     *                        |  |          |  |                             * 
     *   On overhead panel :  |  |   and    |  |                             * 
     *                                                                       * 
     *                      1ST SHOT      2ND SHOT                           * 
     *                                                                       * 
     * IMMEDIATE PROCEDURE :                                                 * 
     *                                                                       * 
     * - During takeoff, in hover or during approach when safe landing is    * 
     *   possible                                                            * 
     *                                                                       * 
     *                         LAND IMMEDIATELY                              * 
     *                                                                       * 
     *   When aircraft is stopped, follow the ground fire procedure          *  
     *   (paragraph 9.3).                                                    * 
     *                                                                       *  
     * - All other flight configurations                                     * 
     *                                                                       * 
     *   Adjust collective pitch and airspeed to allow for an engine         * 
     *   failure.                                                            * 
     *                                                                       * 
     *         Proceed as follows for the engine affected by the fire :      * 
     *                                                                       * 
     *         . Fuel shutoff control............................Pull        * 
     *                                                                       * 
     *           NOTE: As a consequence the FLI display is lost.             *  
     *                                                                       * 
     *         . 1ST SHOT pushbutton light (red, illuminated)....Press       * 
     *         . Start timer                                                 * R 
     *         . Fuel flow control.............................. shutdown    * 
     *                                                           gate        * 
     *         . Booster pumps.................................. Off         * 
     *         . 1ST SHOT pushbutton............................ Check yellow* 
     *                                                           light ON    * 
     *                                                                       * 
     *         If the FIRE light goes out :                                  * 
     *                         LAND AS SOON AS POSSIBLE                      *  
     *                                                                       * 
     *         If the FIRE light does not go out after 1 min approx. :       * R 
     *                                                                       * R 
     *         . 2ND SHOT pushbutton light (red, illuminated)... Press       * 
     *                                                                       * 
     *         If the FIRE light does not go out after 1 minute :            * 
     *                                                                       * 
     *                         LAND IMMEDIATELY                              * 
     *                                                                       * 
     *         After landing, General CUT-OUT Handle                         * 
     *         (Section 3.1 paragraph 2)........................ Pull        *  
     *         Fight the fire from the outside using portable                * 
     *         fire extinguishers if available.                              * 
     * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * *

 *01*
 
  
9.5  Fire Indicating System Failure                                             
  
     - Symptoms : 
                            |         | 
       . Illumination of :  |FIRE DET | 
                            |         | 
     - Procedure : 
  
       . Carry out MGB and engine fire detection system tests. 
  
         If one test is incorrect : 
  
         - LIMIT DURATION OF FLIGHT. 
  
         If all four fire detection tests are OK (confirming a detection 
         line system fault). 
  
         - CONTINUE FLIGHT. 
                                              
     NOTE: If one FIRE test causes |FIRE DET |light off, note which one        R 
           test for post flight maintenance.                                   R

FLIGHT MANUAL 
7  LH HYDRAULIC RESERVOIR LOW FLUID LEVEL
   - Symptoms      : Yellow LVL, SERVO and AP.P lights illuminate on  
                    Hydraulic System Management Panel.  
   - Consequences  : - Loss of one cylinder of tail servocontrol.  
                    - Loss of AP hydraulic system.  
                    - Loss of normal landing gear extension.                     
                    - Wheel and rotor braking limited to the capacity of  
                      the ancillary accumulator.                               R  
   - Procedures    : - Disengage the AFCS units and limit the airspeed to         
                      140 kt.                                                    
                    - APPLY EMERGENCY LANDING GEAR EXTENSION PROCEDURE   
                      (SECTION 3.4 PARAGRAPH 1).  
                    - LAND AS SOON AS POSSIBLE.  
                    Before landing:                                            R  
                    - Ensure that ancillary accumulator pressure is at least   R  
                      120 bars.                                                R  
                    After landing:                                             R  
                    - Monitor the pressure of the ancillary accumulator.       R  
                    CAUTION  : IF THE PRESSURE OF THE ANCILLARY ACCUMULATOR     R  
                              DROPS BELOW 120 BARS, CONSIDER THAT WHEEL AND    R  
                              ROTOR BRAKING IS LOST.                           R  
8  LH HYDRAULIC RESERVOIR LOW FLUID LEVEL AND MAIN HYDRAULIC SYSTEM
   PRESSURE DROP
   - Symptoms      : Yellow LVL, M.P, SERVO and AP.P lights illuminate  
                    on Hydraulic System Management Panel.  
   - Consequences  : - Loss of one cylinder of tail servocontrol.   
                    - Loss of AP hydraulic system.   
                    - Loss of normal landing gear extension.  
                    - Wheel and rotor braking limited to the capacity of  
                      the ancillary accumulator.  
   - Procedures    : - Disengage the AFCS units and limit the airspeed to 140 kt.  
                    - APPLY EMERGENCY LANDING GEAR EXTENSION PROCEDURE  
                      (SECTION 3.4 PARAGRAPH 1).  
                    - LAND AS SOON AS POSSIBLE.  
                    Before landing:                                            R  
                    - Ensure that ancillary accumulator pressure is at least   R  
                      120 bars.                                                R  
                    After landing:                                             R  
                    - Monitor the pressure of the ancillary accumulator.       R  
                    CAUTION  : IF THE PRESSURE OF THE ANCILLARY ACCUMULATOR     R  
                              DROPS BELOW 120 BARS, CONSIDER THAT WHEEL AND    R  
                              ROTOR BRAKING IS LOST.                           R

9 LH HYDRAULIC RESERVOIR LOW FLUID LEVEL AND ZERO PRESSURE IN LH
   HYDRAULIC SYSTEM 
   - Symptoms      : Yellow LVL, M.P, SERVO, AP.P and AUX.P lights   
                    illuminate on Hydraulic System Management Panel.   
   - Consequences  : - Loss of one cylinder of main and tail servocontrols.   
                    - Loss of AP hydraulic system.   
                    - Loss of normal landing gear extension.   
                    - Wheel and rotor braking limited to the capacity of   
                      the ancillary accumulator.                               R  
   - Procedure     : - Disengage the AFCS units and limit the airspeed  
                      to 140 kt.  
                    - Limit the bank angle in turns to 30°.   
                    - AUX.PUMP switch ................................. OFF  
                      Blue OFF light illuminates.   
                    - APPLY EMERGENCY LANDING GEAR EXTENSION PROCEDURE         R  
                      (SECTION 3.4 PARAGRAPH 1).                               R  
                    - LAND AS SOON AS POSSIBLE.  
                    Before landing :                                           R  
                    - Ensure that ancillary accumulator pressure is at least   R  
                      120 bars.                                                R  
                    After landing :                                            R  
                    - Monitor the pressure of the ancillary accumulator.       R  
                    CAUTION  : IF THE PRESSURE OF THE ANCILLARY ACCUMULATOR     R  
                              DROPS BELOW 120 BARS, CONSIDER THAT WHEEL AND    R  
                              ROTOR BRAKING IS LOST.                           R  
10  AUTOPILOT HYDRAULIC SYSTEM MALFUNCTION
    - Symptoms      : Yellow AP.P light illuminates on Hydraulic System           
                     Management Panel.  
    - Consequences  : Pressure drop in AP hydraulic system.  
    - Procedure     : - Disengage the AFCS units and limit the airspeed to  
                       140 kt.  
                     - LIMIT DURATION OF FLIGHT.

- Symptoms Duringnormallandinggearextensioncycle:
Theyellowgeartravelindicatorlightremains

downarrowsdoesnotilluminate.
- Consequences Oneormorelandinggearunitsisnotproperlyextended.

Ifthefailurepersist:
- Procedure - EMERGENCYLANDINGGEAREXTENSIONPROCEDURE:

lighton).
·CheckL/Gcontrolselectortodownposition.
·TheL/Gextends(fullemergencyextensionlastsfor
about2 minutes).
·TheL/Gisextended(3greenarrowsilluminated).

IIlockeddownll conditioncanbe checkedby selectingan lAS<60kt.R
Ifthelandinggearwarningdoesnotcomeon,thelandinggearis R
to be consideredas lockeddown. R
· ReturntheEMERG.L/GEXT.handlecontroltodown
position(greenPUMPlightoff).
NOTE2 Duringemergencylandinggearextension,themainlandinggear R
unitsdonotextendsimultaneously.
NOTE3 Ifoneof thegreengeardownarrowsof themainlandinggear R
stilldoesnotilluminate,aftervisualconfirmationthatall
thewheelsareextended,landwithprecautionandavoidextended
taxiing.(GRD/FLTyellowlightonCautionAdvisoryPanelmay
illuminate).

3 FUELFILTERCLOGGING
Symptoms
Procedure
- YellowFILTlightilluminateson FuelManagementPanel.
- AttempttoextinguishtheFILTlightbythrottling
backtheaffectedengineusingthefuelflowcontrol.
- Ifthelightremainson,shutdowntheengine
(Section3.1).
- LIMITDURATIONOF FLIGHT.
- DONOTTRANSFERFUEL.
R
NOTE: Enginemaybe restartedifnecessarytoensure
-- safelanding.
CAUTION IFYELLOWFILTLIGHTOFTHEOTHERFUELCIRCUITILLUMINATES R
R
- LANDAS SOONAS POSSIBLE. R
4 LOWFUELLEVEL
- Symptoms
- Procedure
Yellowlowfuellevelarrowilluminateson Fuel
ManagementPanel.Fueltransfertooslowduetoaircraft
attitudeor failureof jetpumps.
PresstheLONGIpushbuttonto checkthatlongitudinal
feedertankcontainsabout157lb (90 1) of fuel R
(i.e.15minutesof low-altitudeflightat fastcruising
speed).At highnose-upattitudesthelow-levellightmay
illuminateevenifthecontentsgaugeindicatesa total
fuelquantityexceeding157lb (90 1). R
Dependingon theremainingfuelinthevarioustanks,
operateso thatthequantityindicatedinthefeedertank
doesnotdropbelow110lb (60 1), eitherbytransferring
fuelfromonegroupto theotheror by reducingthe
nose-upattitude(toimprovejetpumpoperation)when
flightconditionspermit.

5 ENGINEFLAMEOUTAFTERPROJECTILEIMPACT
CAUTION1 :DONOTPERFORMX FEEDPROCEDURE
- IFPRESSUREREADINGBECOMESNORMALAGAINWHENBOOSTERPUMPS
ARESWITCHEDON.
- IFFUELCLOGGINGFILTERLIGHTOFOPERATIVEENGINEIS
ILLUMINATED.
- IFFUELPRESSLIGHTOFOPERATIVEENGINEIS ILLUMINATED.
CAUTION2 ONCEX FEEDISACTIVATED,ACTIONONTHEFUELSHUT-OFFLEVER
OFTHEENGINENOTAFFECTEDWILLCAUSESHUTDOWNOF BOTH
ENGINES.
- Symptoms - Engineflameoutdueto fuelstarvation.
- YellowPRESSlightilluminateson FuelManagementPanel
withpressurereadinglessthan2.17psi(0.15bar).
- Procedure - Carryoutengineshutdowndrills(Section3.1
paragraph7). R
Closefuelshutoffvalveof engineaffectedonX FEED
panelby settingrelevantswitchto forwardposition
(guardup).
- Switchon theboosterpumpsandcheckthatpressure
readingisstilllessthan2.17psi (0.15bar).
- ReducepowertomaximumcontinuousDEI.
- X FEEDswitchpressed ON
.X FEEDlightON
Relightengineaffected(Section3.1paragraph8). R
- Limitbankangleinturnsto 30°.
- Do notexceedMaximumContinuousPower.
- IFPRESSlightilluminateson FuelManagementPanel,
reducepowerinorderto extinguishit.
LIMITDURATIONOF FLIGHT

4 t4TEMPERATUREFAULT
Symptoms t4 indicatorreadingabnormallylowor high.
Procedure Confirmthefaultconditionsbymovingcollectivepitch
IfthecorrespondingengineNg andt4bothvaryinthe
samedirection,an enginemalfunctionisprobable.
- Monitoranyadditionalsymptom:checkparameters
consistency.
- Considerengineshutdown.
LIMITDURATIONOF FLIGHT.
5 BLEEDVALVEFAULT
- Symptoms :CASEN°l Green& yellowhatchedBLEEDflagremains
visibleonA Ng indicatorwhenengine
speedis increasedwith:
PRETU 223 : - Ng > 85 ~ (normalthreshold).o
- Ng > 98 ~ (offsetthreshold).o
POSTTU 223:- Ng > 88 ~ (normalthreshold).o
- Ng > 98 ~ (offsetthreshold).o
CASEN°2 Green& yellowhatchedBLEEDflagdoesnot
appearon A Ng indicatorwhenengine
speedisdecreasedwith:
PRETU 223 : - Ng < 81 ~ (normalthreshold).o
- Ng < 95 ~ (offsetthreshold).o
POSTTU 223:- Ng < 84 ~ (normalthreshold).o
- Ng < 95 ~ (offsetthreshold).o
NOTE: NgvaluesaregivenforHp = O andOAT = +15°C.Actualvalues
---- willvaryslightlywithexternalconditions.
ConsequencesCASEN°l Bleedvalvejammedopen: increasedfuel
consumption,powerlimitedtomaximum
continuousrating,increasedthermalfatigue.
CASEN°2 Bleedvalvejammedclosed: increasedsurging
hazard.
- Procedure Modifyflightconditionsaccordingly.Avoidfast
engineaccelerationanddownwindflight.
NOTE Ifoffsetthresholdcannotbe selectedengineisnot
protectedagainstpossiblesurgingincaseof hotairrecycling
duetotailwinds.

FLIGHTMANUAL
Procedure
- DisabletheOEIHIrating,whennotneeded,usingthepushbutton
beneaththecollectivelevergrip.
NOTE If DIFFNG warninglightgoesout(realignmentofengines
duringactiononthecollectivepitchleveror
fuelflowcontrollever)thencomesonagain,theOEIHI
ratingwillbeagainautomaticallyenabled.
Maintainrotorspeedbetween250and275rpm(94-104% NR)
· IncreasethecollectivepitchifNR increases.
· DecreasethecollectivepitchifNRdecreases.
Usetheenginefuelflowcontrolleveronthefaultyenginetoadjust
theenginespeedasnecessary.
· Reducefuelflowto reduceenginespeed.
· Usetheemergencygoverningrangeto increasespeed.
LIMITTHEDURATIONOF FLIGHTandapplythefollowinglandingprocedure
· SetthetorqueindicatorselectortoTORQUE1 (bluelighton)
tomonitorthefaultyenginetorque.
Begindescent:
- Movethefaultyenginefuelflowcontrolto thefastidlegate.
Finalapproach:
- Increasethefaultyenginefuelflowtoobtain20 % torque.
- EnabletheOEIHIrating.
Afterlanding,shutdownthefaultyenginebeforefullyreducingthe
collectivepitchinorderto preventtherotorspeedfromincreasing.
CAUTION:INTHEUNLIKELYCASEOF MAJORFAILUREOF BOTHENGINEGOVERNORSR
(FOREXAMPLE,TOTALELECTRICALPOWERLOSS),THEPROCEDUREIS R
UNCHANGED;HOWEVER,USEONEOFTHETWOFUELFLOWCONTROL R
LEVERSTO CONTROLTHENR RATING,WHEREASTHEOTHERLEVER R
REMAINSINTHEFLIGHTDETENT. R
ONLYPROCEEDBY SMALLSUCCESSIVEACTIONSON COLLECTIVEPITCH R
INORDERTO BEABLETOADJUSTNR CORRECTLY(PARTICULARLY,TO R
BEGINDESCENT). R
INFINALAPPROACH,DISPLAYABOUT270rpm (102%)NR. R
AFTERLANDING,SHUTDOWNTHEENGINEWHICHHASTHEHIGHERNg R
BEFOREDECREASINGTHECOLLECTIVEPITCH,INORDERTOAVOIDANY R
NR INCREASE. R

FLIGHTMANUAL
14.2 OverspeedShutdown
- Symptoms:
· Engine1 orengine2 shutdown.
· InstrumentpanelIOVSPIlightflashes.
- Procedure:
CONTINUESINGLE-ENGINEFLIGHT.FollowinstructionsinSection3.1
paragraph4 (Single-EngineFailure)
NOTEI: Iftheoriginof theoverspeedconditionisuncertain(orif
engineoverspeedshutdownoccurswithoutrotoroverspeed
detection)do notattempttorestarttheenginewithoutprior
visualexaminationof thedrivecouplingbetweenthefree
turbineandtherotor.
NOTE2 Ifnecessary,dependingon thetypeof failureleadingtothe
overspeedshutdown,theenginemaybeoperatedafter
relightingbyusingthefuelflowcontroltoadjustthe
engineratingaccordingto thepowerdemand(referto
paragraph14.1).Beforerelighting,pressthecorresponding
OVSPpushbuttonto resettheoverspeedprotectionsystem
(enginestartingis impossiblewhentheOVSPlightis
flashing).
14.3 MinorGoverningSystemFailure
- Symptoms:
IGOVIlightilluminatesforoneengine,
+ possibleincorrectA Ng Indication,
+ possiblelightingofCHECKLMTmessageanddisappearanceof all
theindicationsandlimitationson theFLI,
+ possibleenginespeedmismatch,resultingin illuminationof the
DIFFNG lightandenablingof theDEIHI Rating. R
- Consequences:
Maximumenginepowerremainsavailable,butgoverningsystemis
impaired.
· Bleedvalveoffsetcontrolmayfailto operate.

3  CHIP DETECTION IN MAIN GEARBOX 
 
   - Symptoms     : Yellow CHIP light illuminates on the XMSN Panel. 
 
   - Consequences : Metal particles present on one or both detectors in MGB    R 
 
   - Procedure    : . Set the detector control unit                             
                      switch to..................................  CHIP PULSE   
 
                    1st POSSIBILITY : The lights goes out.                     R 
                                      . THE FLIGHT MAY BE CONTINUED             
 
                    2nd POSSIBILITY : The light does not go out or it comes on R 
                                      again after a short time in flight.       
                                      . Reduce power, as required ; if          
                                        possible, do not exceed the level       
                                        flight power at Vy.                     
                                      . LIMIT DURATION OF FLIGHT                
 
     NOTE 1 : When the flight is continued after CHIP PULSE activation, check  R 
              the plugs in accordance with the approved maintenance manual     R 
              procedures, after the last flight of the day.                    R 
     
     NOTE 2 : Without intermediate check, a maximum of two CHIP PULSE          R 
              activations is permitted.                                        R 
             
4  INTERMEDIATE GEARBOX OIL OVERHEATING 
 
   - Symptoms     : . Yellow IGB T light illuminates on the XMSN Panel.         
                    . Line test correct (during the test, the IGB T light       
                      should extinguish, then illuminate again). 
 
   - Consequences : Mechanical problem or lubrication fault. 
 
   - Procedure    : . If possible, do not exceed the level flight power 
                      at Vy. 
 
                      LAND AS SOON AS POSSIBLE. 
 
     NOTE : If the line test is not correct,                                    
 
                      CONTINUE FLIGHT                                           
 
5  TAIL GEARBOX OIL OVERHEATING 
 
   - Symptoms     : . Yellow TGB T light illuminates on the XMSN Panel.         
                    . Line test correct (during the test, the TGB T light       
                      should extinguish, then illuminate again). 
 
   - Consequences : Mechanical problem or lubrication fault. 
 
   - Procedure    : . If possible, do not exceed the level flight power 
                      at Vy. 
 
                      LAND AS SOON AS POSSIBLE. 
 
     NOTE : If the line test is not correct,                                    
 
                      CONTINUE FLIGHT

5  ESSENTIAL BUS SHORT-CIRCUIT 
  
   - Symptoms     : . Yellow ESS BUS, battery +, TRU 2 and BUS TIE lights 
                      illuminate on Electrical System Control Panel. 
                    . Green EMERG.BAT FLOW light comes on. 
  
   - Consequences : 
     . Yellow ENG, PITOT, AIR COND, FIRE DET, WSCRN, lights illuminate on 
       Caution Advisory Panel and captions corresponding to lost equipment 
       on different system panels. 
  
     . TRU 2 line contactors open. 
     . BUS TIE line contactor opens. 
     . Battery + contactor opens. 
  
     . Loss of the following circuits : 
       . P2 electrovalve de-icing              . Autopilot hydraulic cut-out 
       . Display screen cooling fans (2ND      . ADF 2 
         and BACK-UP)                          . TACAN 
       . Individual map lights                 . Landing light 
       . Anticollision light                   . Cockpit slaved valve de-icing 
       . Cockpit green neon dome light         . Cabin slaved valve de-icing 
       . Radioaltimeter 2                      . Pilot's landing light control 
       . DC/FAIL alarm                         . NVG supply 
       . Aux.pump and aux. pump control        . Side windshield wipers 
       . LH & RH hydraulic level (detect.)     . Windshield washer 
       . V/UHF 2                               . Engine 1 and 2 overspeed 
       . RMI                                   . Normal L/G extension control 
       . RH pitot head total ports heating     . SMD 2 
       . Pitot head static ports heating       . SMD 4 
         control                               . ARMS 
       . AFCP 2                                . SLVG ARM 
       . Multipurpose Air Intake 2             . Cabin strip highting 
       . No 2 Fuel transfer pump               . Landing gear indicating 
       . NBC protection suit supply            . DOPPLER 
       . ELIPS decoy dispenser                 . GPS 
       . NADIR II system                       . HF/SSB 
       . Engine 1 and 2 starting               . Optical sight 
       . Sling                                 . Gun 
       . APU governor                          . Utility socket 
       . GRMU                                  . Emergency landing gear 
       . Radar warning receiver                  extending control 
       . APU                                   . APU starter 
       . APU fire detection-extinguisher       . Cabin air conditioning 
       . DF 931                                  control 
       . VOR 2 
  
- Procedure : 
  
     Proceed in the following order : 
     . BAT switch for EMERGENCY CUT-OFF bar .................. Aft 
     . TRU 2 pushbutton ...................................... OFF 
     . BUS TIE switch ........................................ OPEN 
     . Extend the landing gear by emergency procedure 
       (Section 3.4 paragraph 1) 
  
                           LAND AS SOON AS POSSIBLE

6  SECONDARY BUS SHORT-CIRCUIT 
 
   - Symptoms     : . Yellow SEC BUS, TRU 1, BUS TIE lights illuminate on 
                      Electrical System Control Panel. 
 
   - Consequences : 
 
     . ENG, PITOT, WSCRN and HYD (on ground only) lights illuminate on 
       Caution Advisory Panel and captions corresponding to lost equipment on 
       different system panels. 
     . TRU 1 line contactor opens. 
     . BUS TIE line contactor opens. 
     . Loss of the following circuits : 
 
       . Position lights                       . VOR 1 
       . Vertical gyro GV 76                   . V/UHF 1 
       . PSU 1 (yellow GOV light illuminated   . Center windshield de-icing 
         on engine 1)                          . Center windshield wiper 
       . LH pitot head total ports heating     . Engine 2 booster pump (3) 
       . Copilot's utility light               . SMD 1 
       . LH and RH air ambulance connectors    . SMD 3 
       . Engine 1 booster pump (1)             . WEATHER RADAR 
       . MPAI 1 (LH)                           . IR landing light 
       . No. 1 Fuel transfer pump              . Winch 
       . Cabin strip lighting                  . Gun 
       . Auxiliary hydraulic pump control      . SLVG ARMT 
       . Air to air refuelling probe           . PLS 
       . Fire control unit                     . AFCP 1 
       . Cockpit air conditioning control      . Radio alitimeter 1 
       . FLIR                                  . SPECTROLAB searchlight        R 
 
   - Procedure    : 
 
     Proceed in the following order : 
     . TRU 1 pushbutton ........................... OFF 
     . BUS TIE switch ............................. OPEN 
 
                           LIMIT DURATION OF FLIGHT

7  FAILURE OF ONE TRU 
 
   - Symptoms : 
     . Yellow TRU 1 or TRU 2 lights illuminate on Electrical System Control 
       Panel. 
 
   - Consequences : 
     . The whole system continues to be supplied by the remaining transformer- 
       rectifier. 
     . The SPECTROLAB searchlight is automatically off-loaded. 
 
   - Procedure : 
     . Try to reset TRU 1 or TRU 2 
     If the fault condition disappears 
                                CONTINUE FLIGHT 
  
     If the fault condition persists 
     . Set TRU 1 or TRU 2 pushbutton light ............ OFF 
     . Set FLIR control switch ........................ OFF                     
       .. Armament system control switch .............. OFF                     
       .. Cabin lighting control switch ............... OFF                     
       .. Map light switches .......................... OFF                     
       .. Cockpit strip lighting potentiometer ........ OFF                     
     . Equipment connected to cockpit sanitary plugs.. Disconnect             
 
      For aircraft subject to mid-life upgrade, set the following equipment     
      as follows :                                                              
 
     . Video Recorder.................................. OFF                     
     . Downlink........................................ OFF                     
     . BARCO mission display unit...................... OFF                     
     . Central windscreen wipers....................... OFF                    R 
                                                                               R 
                                                                               R 
  
                     LIMIT DURATION OF FLIGHT 
 
    NOTE : If failure occurs within the first hour of flight, SHED such 
           circuits as to maintain the load of TRU 1 or TRU 2 below 50%.

 8  FAILURE OF ONE ALTERNATOR 
  
   - Symptoms     : 
  
     . Yellow ALT 1 or ALT 2 light and green Transfer light illuminate on 
       Electrical System Control Panel. 
  
   - Consequences : 
  
     . All systems continue to be supplied by the remaining alternator. 
  
   - Procedure    : 
  
     . Cockpit and cabin air-conditioning ................... OFF 
       Cooling pushbutton lights ............................ OFF 
     . Try to reset alternator 1 or 2 
  
     If the fault condition persists : 
  
     . Set ALT 1 or ALT 2 pushbutton light to ............... OFF 
  
                                CONTINUE FLIGHT 
  
     NOTE : Shed if necessary as to maintain the load of the remaining 
            alternator below 100 %. 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
                                                                               R

 9  FAILURE OF BOTH ALTERNATORS 
  
   - Symptoms     : 
  
     . Yellow ALT 1, ALT 2, BUS 1, BUS 2, TRU 1, TRU 2, SEC BUS and BUS TIE 
       lights illuminate on Electrical System Control Panel. 
     . Red DC light illuminates on Caution Advisory Panel. 
  
   - Consequences : 
  
     . ENG, PITOT, WSCRN and HYD (on ground only) lights illuminate on 
       Caution Advisory Panel and captions corresponding to lost equipment on 
       different system panels. 
     . Loss of AC power consumers. 
     . Loss of transformer-rectifiers, and DC consumers of the secondary 
       bus bar. 
     . Essential circuits remain supplied by main battery, until the APU 
       generation is operative. 
  
   - Procedure    : 
  
     . Try to reset both alternators. 
  
     If the fault condition persists : 
  
     With APU :                                                                 
  
     . Set ALT 1 and ALT 2 pushbutton lights to ......................... OFF 
     . Switch off cabin and cockpit air conditioning systems and both 
       MPAI fans. 
     . Start up and engage the APU. 
     . The cabin and cockpit air conditioning systems remain off-loaded 
       during emergency mode flight on the APU. 
     . Select back ON the equipment previously intentionally selected OFF. 
     . If necessary the MPAI fans may be operated for 5 minutes for landing. 
     . Set DOWNLINK (For aircraft subject to mid-life upgrade)... ....... OFF   R 
  
     NOTE : The APU system is covered by SUP.13.                                
  
     Without APU :                                                              
  
     . Set ALT 1 and ALT 2 pushbutton lights to ......................... OFF   
     . Reduce altitude below 5000 ft.                                           
     . Reduce Ng according to chart opposite.                                   
     . Extend the landing gear by normal procedure.                             
     . Reduce electrical consumption.                                           
     . Carefully monitor the battery voltage.                                   
  
                            LAND AS SOON AS POSSIBLE                            
  
  
     CAUTION : IF THE BATTERY VOLTAGE DROPS TO 22 VOLTS, A TOTAL ELECTRICAL     
               FAILURE MAY BE IMMINENT. THE AIRCRAFT SHOULD BE PLACED IN A      
 
 
 
 
 
 
                                                                               R

10  FAILURE OF BOTH TRUs 
  
    - Symptoms : 
  
      . Red DC light illuminates on Caution Advisory Panel. 
      . Yellow TRU 1, TRU 2, SEC BUS, BUS TIE illuminate on Electrical 
        System Control Panel. 
  
    - Consequences : 
  
      . ENG, PITOT, WSCRN and HYD (on ground only) lights illuminate on 
        Caution Advisory Panel and captions corresponding to lost equipment on 
        different system panels. 
      . Loss of DC consumers of the secondary bus bar. 
      . Essential circuits remain supplied by main battery.                     
      . Remaining flying time greater than or equal to 20 min. or 19 min.      R 
        (For aircraft subject to mid-life upgrade) with intentional shedding   R 
        of the following circuits :                                            R 
  
        . Engine 1 RH booster pump (2)      . Tacan                             
        . Engine 2 RH booster pump (4)      . Nadir                             
        . AFCP 1 and 2                      . GPS                               
        . SMD 2 and 4                       . Doppler                           
        . DCU 1 and 2                       . Instrument panel, console and     
        . RH pitot head static ports heating  subpanel lighting                R 
        . Heating - ventilation             . Map light                         
        . AFCS 1 and 2                      . Cockpit and cabin green lighting  
        . FDC 1                             . Guns-rockets weapon system        
        . Side windscreen wiper                                                R 
  
    - Procedure : 
  
      . Try to reset both TRUs. 
  
      If the fault condition persists : 
  
      . Set TRU 1 and TRU 2 to.................................... OFF. 
      . Reduce altitude below 5000 ft.                                          
      . Reduce Ng according to chart opposite.                                  
      . Extend the landing gear by normal procedure.                            
      . Reduce electrical consumption.                                          
      . Carefully monitor the battery voltage.                                  
  
      Limit the use of the following equipment items during landing phase :    R 
 
      . Side windscreen wiper.....................................5 min.       R 
      . V/UHF 2 COLLINS ARC 210..................................10 min.       R 
      . Landing light.............................................5 min.       R 
      . Engine 1 and 2 booster pumps 1 and 3......................5 min.       R 
 
                               LAND AS SOON AS POSSIBLE                         
  
  
   CAUTION : IF THE BATTERY VOLTAGE DROPS TO 22 VOLTS, A TOTAL ELECTRICAL       
             FAILURE MAY BE IMMINENT. THE AIRCRAFT SHOULD BE PLACED IN A        
             CONFIGURATION THAT MAY BE FLOWN AT 85 % Ng. ONLY THE LH            
             NR INDICATOR WILL REMAIN OPERABLE TO CONTROL THE ENGINES IN        
             THE EMERGENCY GOVERNING MODE.                                      
             THE INTERCOM SYSTEM WILL NOT OPERATE.                              
                                                                               R

13  EMERGENCY BATTERY FAILURE 
  
    - Symptoms     : 
  
      . Yellow E.BAT light illuminates on Electrical System Control Panel. 
  
    - Consequence  : 
  
      . Loss of the functions ensured by the emergency battery, i.e. : 
        - Short-circuit monitoring on primary and secondary buses in the 
          DC master box. 
        - Emergency supply to the standby artificial horizon. 
        - Emergency supply to the emergency lighting system and to the 
          passenger notices panels. 
  
    - Procedure    :       LIMIT DURATION OF FLIGHT                             
                                                                               R

 *01*
 
14  ABNORMAL VOLTAGE INDICATION 
  
   - Symptoms     : DC or AC needle voltmeter in the yellow arc. 
  
   - Consequences : None. 
                    This case is taken into account by the electrical 
                    system protection. 
  
   - Procedure    : Prepare for possible alternator failure (Section 3.8 
                    paragraph 8) or TRU (Section 3.8 paragraph 7) failure.

FLIGHT MANUAL 
1 GENERAL
   The Integrated Flight Display System (IFDS) is an integrated digital
   dual-pilot system comprising two identical half-systems operating
   independently with provision for reconfiguration in the event of failure
   of one half-system.
Navigation and Mission Display (NMD)  Primary Flight Display (PFD)
Zone 1 : Compass card                 Zone 1  : Attitude display
Zone 2 : Navigation status indicator  Zone 1a : Fault messages and warnings
Zone 3 : Message zone                           indicator
Zone 4 : Radio-altimeter indicator    Zone 2  : AFCS status indicator
Zone 5 : Pointer identification       Zone 3  : Category 2 status indicator
         indicator                    Zone 4  : Vertical speed indicator
Zone 6 : Scratch pad                  Zone 5  : Barometric altitude indicator
Zone 7 : Wind indicator               Zone 6  : DH/MDA indicator
Zone 8 : Reconfiguration status       Zone 7  : Magnetic heading indicator
         indicator                    Zone 8  : ILS indicator
Zone 9 : First limit indicator        Zone 9  : Reconfiguration status
Zone 10: Cooling fan fault indicator            indicator
                                      Zone 10 : Airspeed indicator
                                      Zone 11 : Cooling fan fault indicator
                                      Zone 12 : First limit indicator

FLIGHTMANUAL
8 FLIGHTDATASUBSYSTEMFAILURES
8.1 TotalFailureof OneFlightDataSubsystem
- Symptoms On PFDandNMDaffectedby faultcondition
· RedFDC1 or FDC2 messageappears,andrelated
symbolsdisappear:attitude,barometricaltitude,
magneticheading,verticalspeed,airspeedand
compasscard.
- On PFO1 andPFD2
· RedCHECKATTmessageappears,confirmingloss
of attitudedisplayredundancy.
·AmberCHECKVNEmessageappears.
- On NMD1 andNMD2
·AmberCHECKLMTmessageappears.
- Incaseof FDC2 failure,radiomagneticheading
displayalsodisappears.
NOTE Thisfaultconditionalsoresultsinimpairment
of theAFCSfunctionuntilthereconfiguration.
Refertoparagraph10.3.
- Procedure Reconfigure:
· FaultyFOCpushbuttonon SORU OFF.
Consequences AmberFDC1 or FOC2 messageappearsoutlinedin
reconfigurationzoneof all4 displays.
SelectedFDCdataiscopiedtootherdisplay.
- CHECKATTmessagedisappearson PFD1 andPFD2.
- CHECKLMTandCHECKVNEmessagesremainpresented.
- ForIMCconditions,redundancyisprovidedby standby
indicators.
- ForIFRoperationlASislimitedto 140kt. R
CAUTION IFREALIGNMENTOFTHEFAULTYFDCISATTEMPTED,ALLDATAFROM
THEUNITBEINGREALIGNEDARELOST,DURINGTHEWHOLEREALIGNMENT
PHASEANDTHEMESSAGESINDICATINGTHEFDCFAILUREAPPEAR.

10.4 AutomaticFlightControlSystemComputerFailure
- Symptoms
- Procedure
.. Faultycomputerisautomaticallydisconnected.
Allfouraxisidentifiersilluminateingreen,and
thenumberof thefaultyAFCScomputerilluminatesin
amberto indicatelossof anAFCShalf-system.
.CHECKVNEandCHECKLMTappearon PFDsandNMOs.
.DISlightof thecomputeraffectedbythefault
illuminatesonAFCAU.
NOTE: Referto Failureof SecondAFCS(Paragraph16.1).
TrytoreengagetheAFCS.
10.5 TrimActuatorFailure
- Symptoms
- Procedure
- RedC TRIM,y TRIM,R TRIMor P TRIMmessage
appearson PFD.
- Correspondingaxisidentifierilluminatesinamber
- Insomecases(ifonlyoneAFCSunitoroneAFCP
unitisoperating)therecovermanualcontrolsignal
flasheson therelevantaxis.
- Recovermanualcontrol
- Reconfigure:
. Foryaw,rollandpitchaxes:
..AUTOTRIMpushbuttonof defective
axisonAFCAU OFF
. Forcollectiveaxis:
..TR FEELCallpushbuttononAFCAU to REL
ConsequencesRedalarmmessagedisappears.
AFCSremainsoperational,butcontrolauthoritymaybe
restricted.Thissituationis indicatedbya OUTOF
TRIMcondition:referto paragraph12.
NOTE Failurewarningmessagesmaybecombinedforseveralaxes
(e.g.CYTRIMmessageforsimultaneouscollectiveandyawtrim
actuatorfailures).
CAUTION: - IFTHEREDP TRIMOR R TRIMMESSAGEREAPPEARS,THE
FAULTYAXISMUSTBE ISOLATEDBY PRESSINGTHECYCLIC
TRIMFEELSWITCHONAFCAU.
- IFTHEY TRIMMESSAGEREAPPEARS,THEPILOTMUSTKEEP
HISFEETONTHEYAWPEDALS.

10.8 ILS failure                                                               R
     - Symptoms      : Red LOC - G/S message appears on PFD in area 8.          R
     - Procedure     : Avoid ILS approach.                                      R
11 DISCREPANCY MESSAGES
11.1 FDC Discrepancy
     - Symptoms      : - Red recover manual control warning flashes for
                        all four axes on system status line of AFCS indicator.
                      - Amber FDC DISC message appears on PFD.
                      - In some cases, amber PSU DISC message appears on
                        PFD.
                      - Amber CHECK VNE message appears on both PFDs.
     - Procedure     : - Recover manual control.
                      - Determine which half-system is accurate by comparison
                        with standby artificial horizon.
                      - Reconfigure
                        Faulty FDC (1 or 2) pushbutton on SDRU.......... OFF.
     - Consequences  : - Recover manual control warning disappears.
                      - FDC DISC message disappears.
                      - Amber FDC1 or FDC2 message illuminates in
                        reconfiguration zone.
                      - FDC data obtained from operating unit.
                      - CHECK LMT and CHECK VNE messages are presented.
                      - The performance of the autopilot is slightly impaired
                        and OUT OF TRIM symbols may appear.

FLIGHTMANUAL
3 FAILUREOF THEANTI-ICINGCIRCUITOF THESTATICPRESSUREPORTSINONE
PITOTHEAD
- Symptoms YellowFIor F2 lightilluminateson subpanel.
Consequences Possiblyinaccurateairdatasignalsin icingconditions.
- Procedure Switchoffstaticportheatingsystem.
. Switchto standbyinteriorstaticportsandapply
correctionsto altitudeandairspeedsignalsfromair
datasystem.
4 FAILUREOF GROUND/FLIGHTSWITCHINGCIRCUITON ONEPITOTHEAD
- Symptoms .. YellowFIor F2 light(STATIC)andLHor RH FAIL
lightilluminateon subpanelandinsomecases,
yellowGRD/FLTlightonCautionadvisorypanel.
· Lossof theheatingsystemof thetotalandstatic
portsintheLHor RH Pitothead.
- Procedure Switchofftheheatingsystemforthetotalportsof
thefaultyPitotheadandforstaticports.
· Reconfigureon thePSUrelatedto thePitotheadfor
whichtheanti-icingsystemcontinuestooperate
PSUswitchon SDRU Settothesideof
validPSU
· Switchto standbyinteriorstaticportsandapply
correctionsto altitudeandairspeedsignalsfrom air
datasystem.
- Consequences SeeparagraphGENERAL
NOTE Incaseof failureof theground/flightsignalfrombothPitot
headsas indicatedby airdatasignalerrors(thereisnoother
meansofdetection),totalandstaticpressureportsheating
systemsaresuppliedat partialpower: iceformationmaythereforeR
occurinicingatmosphere.

Consequence

lossof thecockpitheating/demistingsystem.

Symptom
Consequence
Procedure
YellowREGlightilluminateson thecontrolpanel.
Theautomatictemperatureregulationsystemisno longer
operating(mixedairtemperatureaboveBO°C).

within15seconds.Otherwisesetthe7
positionselectortoOFFto resetthesystem,
thensetpushbuttonto MANU
- Adjustthetemperaturemanually,
usingtheHOT-COLDcontrol.
CONTINUEFLIGHT

mixedairtemperaturedropsbelow75°C.

shutofengineP2bleedvalve.

4 AURALWARNING
Theauralwarningsignalisaudibleintheintercomheadphonesinthe
followingcircumstances
- RotorSpeedWarning
· Rotorunderspeedwarningrange: 200to 249rpm (75,5to94%).
· Rotoroverspeedwarninglimit :290rpm (109,5%).
- MainGearboxor EngineBayFireWarning
·Theauralwarningsignalisactuatedsimultaneouslywithillumination
of theMGBFIRElightsforthemaingearbox,or theFIRE1 or
FIRE2 lightfortheengines.
- MasterWarning R
As soonas theMasterwarninglightcomeson,a shortauralwarning R
signalisaudible. R
- LandingGearWarning
Theauralwarningsignalisaudibleifthelandinggearisnotextended
whentheairspeedis60 knots(110km/h)or lessandtheaircraft
heightis300ft (90m) or lower.
Theauralwarningsignalisaudiblefor3 seconds.
R
R
- DecisionHeight
.DHwarningsignal(radioaltimetric)
Audiblewhenthehelicopter,descending,crossestheselected
decisionheight.
.MDA/HorDHAwarningsignal
Audiblewhenthehelicopter,descending,crossestheselected
decisionheight.
Theauralwarningsignalmaybe switchedoff(andautomaticallyreset) R
bypressingthesubpanelAURALpushbutton(exceptfordecisionheight). R

4  CHECKS BEFORE STARTING ENGINES 
  
   Ensure that aircraft performance and limitations are consistent with the 
   planned flight conditions, and check that aircraft weight and balance 
   are within approved limits. 
  
   - Seats .................................................. Adjusted, 
                                                              latched, checked 
   - Harnesses .............................................. Adjusted, secure 
   - Pedals ................................................. Adjusted 
   - Doors .................................................. Closed, safety 
                                                              system locked 
                                                              (indicators out 
                                                              of sight), 
                                                              lights OK. 
 
   NOTE : All emergency doors must be closed, locked but not locked by key.    R 
 
   - Utility hydraulic accumulator .......................... Needle in the 
                                                              green arc 
   - Parking brake .......................................... Applied 
   - Landing gear emergency extension handle ................ Lowered, guarded 
   - Nose wheel castoring lock .............................. As necessary 
   - Landing gear control ................................... Safety pin 
                                                              removed 
   - Emergency electrical cut-off gangbar ................... Snapwired, 
                                                              forward 
   - Cockpit and cabin heating and ventilation systems ...... Off 
  
   - Switches and pushbuttons : 
     . BAT .................................................. ON 
       - Battery voltage .................................... 25 V minimum 
       - After self-test of engine computers : 
         GOV (engine 1 or 2) - PWR 1 - PWR 2 lights ......... Off               
     . TRU 1 + TRU 2 ........................................) Press : 
     . ALT 1 + ALT 2 ........................................) white light off 
     . BUS TIE .............................................. to NORM 
     . EMERG.BAT ............................................ to NORM 
     . GPU .................................................. As required 
     . WARN and ALARM ....................................... Rearmed 
     . APU .................................................. As required       
                       
   NOTE: If |FIRE DET |flashes carry out MGB and ENGINE FIRE tests and note     
         which one test causes |FIRE DET |light off for post flight             
         maintenance.

5  ENGINE STARTING 
  
5.1  Engine Starting Information 
  
     - Engine starting sequence : either engine may be started first. 
  
     - Residual t4 before engine starting :                                     
     
       . If the residual t4 exceeds 100°C, refer to the paragraph 5.4           
         "Hot engine starting procedure".                                       
  
     - Starter operation : 
  
       After three consecutive starting attempts or cranking operations, 
       wait at least 20 minutes before trying again. 
  
     - Cranking applications : 
  
       Crank engine after unsuccessful engine start or for various engine 
       checks. 
  
     - Cranking procedure : 
  
       . Fuel flow control .......................... Off 
       . CRANK switch ............................... Press for 15 s maximum, 
                                                      check rise in Ng  
                                                     
     CAUTION : DO NOT CRANK ENGINE WITH FUEL SHUTOFF VALVE CLOSED.

5.2  Starting the First (1 or 2) Engine 
  
     - Cyclic stick ..................................... Check centered 
                                                          on visual marks 
     - Collective pitch lever ........................... Full low pitch, 
                                                          secure in position 
     - Booster pumps .................................... On 
       . PRESS light .................................... Off 
       . Fuel pressure .................................. Check 
     - Fuel flow control lever .......................... Starting detent 
       . OIL P1 or OIL P2 light ......................... Illuminated 
       . ALARM light .................................... Illuminated. Rearm 
       . WARN light ..................................... Illuminated. Rearm 
     - Cockpit and cabin air-conditioning ............... Off 
       . Cooling pushbutton lights ...................... Off 
     - Residual t4 ...................................... Check                R 
     - Starting pushbutton .............................. Press & release 
  
       . The t4 must remain within the general starting limitations.           R 
         Refer to the section 2.1                                              R 
  
     CAUTION 1 : IMMEDIATELY SHUT DOWN ENGINE IF ROTOR DOES NOT START         
                 ROTATING WHEN Ng REACHES 17 %.                         
                                                                |      |    
     CAUTION 2 : IMMEDIATELY SHUT DOWN ENGINE IF POWER LIGHT    | PWR1 |   OR   
                                                                |      |      
              |      |         
              | PWR2 |   COMES ON AND DOES NOT EXTINGUISH AT STARTER CUT-OFF.  R 
              |      |                                                     
  
       . S light illuminates, then goes out at 45% Ng. 
       . Engine 1 & 2 OEI HI lights illuminate on Δ Ng indicator. 
       . OIL P1 or OIL P2 light goes out when pressure reaches 24.70 psi 
         (1.7 bar). 
       . OVSP light goes out when rotor speed is approx 60 rpm (23%) 
       . MGB P light goes out. 
       . M P and RH P lights go out. 
  
     - Fuel flow control lever .......................... Forward position 
                                                          (slowly) 
       . Engine acceleration consistent with the lever displacement speed. 
       . Aural warning when NR is between 200 & 249 rpm (75.5 - 94 %). 
       . Alternators coupled when rotor speed is approx 245 rpm (92.5 %). 
       . ELEC light goes out. 
       . HYD light goes out. 
       . XMSN light goes out (M P and S/B P lights go out on XMSN panel). 
  
     - OEI LO ratings for Engines 1 & 2 ................. Armed 
       . OEI LO lights .................................. On 
  
     - Auxiliary hydraulic pump ......................... On 
  
     - Overhead panel warning lights .................... All off (GPU if 
                                                          applicable) 
  
     - Electric power generating systems ................ Proper voltage 
                                                          and amperage

     NOTE 3 : If the screen indicates that a required equipment item is 
              switched off, simply switch on the unit and the test will 
              continue. 
  
     NOTE 4 : After switching on an AFCS or a display unit, wait at least

              the light comes on but the test does not run. Press the

7  TAXIING 
  
   - AFCS ................................................... DIS lights on 
  
   - Parking brake .......................................... Released 
  
   - Nose wheel ............................................. Unlocked 
  
   - Taxi    : Increase the collective pitch as necessary to 2 or 3 ; move the 
               cyclic stick slightly forward if necessary. 
               Then center the cyclic stick and reduce the collective pitch 
               as appropriate. 
  
   - Turning : Gradually press the yaw pedals ; use the differential wheel 
               brakes for sharper turns. 
               For spot turns at high gross weight, do not begin turning 
               the fuselage before increasing the collective pitch to ease 
               weight off the nose gear. 
  
   - Stopping : Move the cyclic stick to neutral position and the collective 
                lever to minimum ; gradually apply wheel brakes. 
                Do not apply the parking brake until the aircraft comes 
                to a full stop. 
  
  
     CAUTION : 1. THE AIRCRAFT WILL TEND TO BANK OUTSIDE A TURN, ESPECIALLY 
                  IN A CROSSWIND. COMPENSATE FOR THIS MOTION USING THE CYCLIC 
                  STICK AND BY INCREASING THE COLLECTIVE PITCH IF NECESSARY. 
  
               2. LIMIT CYCLIC STICK MOVEMENTS AWAY FROM THE NEUTRAL 
                  POSITION TO ABOUT 1 in (2.5 cm) FORE-&-AFT AND 2 in (5 cm) 
                  LATERALLY, ESPECIALLY AT MINIMUM COLLECTIVE PITCH SETTING, 
                  TO PREVENT ROTOR BLADES FROM STRIKING DROOP RESTRAINERS. 
  
               3. ON GROUND, IN ORDER TO PREVENT ANY RISK OF VERTICAL          R 
                  OSCILLATIONS, THE TRIM LOADS MUST BE RELEASED BY DEPRESSING  R 
                  THE COLLECTIVE TRIM RELEASE TRIGGER WHENEVER THE COLLECTIVE  R 
                  LEVER IS MOVED.                                              R

8  TAKEOFF 
  
8.1  Checks Prior to Takeoff 
 
     Aircraft equipped with ELECTRONIC WARFARE SYSTEM (EWS) :                  R 
     Refer to SUP.45.                                                          R 
 
     Aircraft equipped with ELIPS Decoy Dispenser System :                     R 
 
     Test the ELIPS Decoy Dispenser System (According to Mission)            
 
     1) Check the circuits : 
        - Check MASS switch ...................... OFF 
  
        - L-BOTH-R selector switch ............... Set to select the desired    
                                                   decoy dispenser 
  
        - Control unit switch .................... ON : Self-test activated 
                                                   . INOP light illuminates 
                                                     3 s, then goes out. 
                                                   . Firing sequence number 
                                                     appears in SET window. 
                                                   . Letters dA flash 30 s 
                                                     in EM window. 
                                                   . Number 24 flashes in EM 
                                                     and IR windows (if both 
                                                     dispensers installed) on 
                                                     completion of self-test. 
  
     2) Check the cartridges 
  
        CAUTION : THIS CHECK REQUIRES THE MASS SWITCH TO BE SET TO ON. 
                  THEREFORE, TAKE ALL NECESSARY PRECAUTIONS TO PREVENT ANY 
                  ACCIDENT IN THE EVENT OF UNTIMELY FIRING. 
  
        - FIRING pushbutton on selection panel ... Guard down 
  
        - Firing override controls on cyclic 
          stick grips ............................ Guard down 
  
        - MASS switch ............................ ON                           
                                                   . Number of available 
                                                     cartridges in each 
                                                     dispenser displayed in 
                                                     EM and IR windows. 
        - MASS switch ............................ OFF                          
                                                   . Number 24 flashes in EM 
                                                     and IR windows (standby 
                                                     position). 
 
        - Flight performance limitations ............ Known 
  
        - V1 and climbing speed ..................... Announced 
  
        - Caution Advisory Panel .................... All lights off 
  
        - Pressure and temperature readings ......... Checked 
          . Indicator pointers ...................... In green range

     Anytime it is necessary to protect engines against sand, snow, 
     hailstone or ice ingestion, proceed as follows :  
                       
     In sand-laden atmosphere : 
     - MPAI SAND FILTERS switch ............... AUTO L/G or CONTINUOUS

                                                   . FAN FAIL lights off

8.2  Hovering 
  
     - If AUW > 20460 lb (9300 kg) and/or H σ > 7200 ft, 
       NR + 10 ................................... ON 
                                                   Check NR increases 
  
     - AFCS ...................................... Engaged 
       . AFCS status indicator ................... Blank 
  
     - Parking brake ............................. As required 
  
     - Nose wheels ............................... Locked on centerline 
  
     - Gradually ease weight off wheels vertically (see CAUTION) 
  
     - Check the following : 
       . The red light DIFF NG does not illuminate during engine 
         acceleration. 
  
     - Stabilize hover at 10 ft 
  
     - Check the following : 
  
       . Power margin (1st limitation) 
       . Torque, Ng, t4, pressure and temperature readings 
       . Caution Advisory Panel 
       . Rotor rpm 
       . ADIs 
  
     CAUTION : GROUND RESONANCE MAY OCCUR WHEN AIRCRAFT IS MAINTAINED ON THE 
               GROUND WITH WEIGHT PARTIALLY OFFSET BY ROTOR LIFT. 
               THE OSCILLATIONS CAN BE STOPPED BY TAKING OFF, BY REDUCING THE 
               COLLECTIVE PITCH OR BY DISCONNECTING AFCS.
